# Enchanting — recipe candidates

**43 cards; reviewed authored-operational PLAYTEST with AEX-01–07 fulfilled.** Sources and original book prose remain preserved.

## Refined Tide Enchanted Armor — recipe-121

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +1, maximum 1 charge(s), and cold as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 cold to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved cold damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 cold, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-armor: Existing Standard Medium chain shirt default; other registered ordinary armor suits retain their exact source body. 50 gp, 20 lb, ordinary B15, structural AC 19, dimensions 30 × 22 × 6 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 6 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 4; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 8. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 4 hours; operational 240 active minutes, or 180 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 225/165 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 9, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 2; Standard 4; Exceptional yield 5; Masterwork yield 6. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 4 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Luminous Crystal (ing-121-0-luminous_crystal): quantity 2; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [1] bronze dragon rib (drop-bronze_dragon_rib): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 96 gp / 0.58 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-armor: finished unit 359/4 gp, functional repair material 25/2 gp and default 3/2 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 359/16 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 8. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Imbues one compatible item with a +1 enhancement and 1 charge. Spend a charge to produce a water effect appropriate to the item; charges return at dawn.

An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 cold to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved cold damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 cold, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-wand: Handheld hardwood conductor, no ordinary weapon or spell-focus grant. 1 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 12 × 1 × 1 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 7 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 3; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 12. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 8, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 2; Standard 5; Exceptional yield 6; Masterwork yield 7. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 5 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Stabilizing matrix (ing-124-0-stabilizing_matrix): quantity 4; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role stabilizer; consumed=true.
- [1] Luminous Crystal (ing-124-1-luminous_crystal): quantity 2; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [2] Binding Glyphs (ing-124-2-binding_glyphs): quantity 1; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.
- [3] bronze dragon rib (drop-bronze_dragon_rib): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 96 gp / 0.58 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-wand: finished unit 213/5 gp, functional repair material 1/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 213/20 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 12. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 psychic to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved psychic damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 psychic, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 2; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 12. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 8 hours; operational 480 active minutes, or 360 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 465/345 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. **Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Retention and failure**

On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 psychic to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved psychic damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 psychic, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-charm: Small handled steel token, no passive wearing effect. 5 gp, 0.1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 2 × 1 × 0.25 inches; EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches; EN-armor: Existing Standard Medium chain shirt default; other registered ordinary armor suits retain their exact source body. 50 gp, 20 lb, ordinary B15, structural AC 19, dimensions 30 × 22 × 6 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 1; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 8. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 8 hours; operational 480 active minutes, or 360 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 465/345 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 6, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Luminous Crystal (ing-130-0-luminous_crystal): quantity 2; tier 1; source unit 2 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [1] Binding Glyphs (ing-130-1-binding_glyphs): quantity 5; tier 1; source unit 2 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.
- [2] Stabilizing matrix (ing-130-2-stabilizing_matrix): quantity 1; tier 1; source unit 2 gp / 0.1 lb; role stabilizer; consumed=true.
- [3] mechanical eye (drop-mechanical_eye): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 84 gp / 0.38 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 3. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-charm: finished unit 144 gp, functional repair material 5/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 36 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 8; EN-weapon: finished unit 154 gp, functional repair material 15/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 77/2 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 8; EN-armor: finished unit 189 gp, functional repair material 25/2 gp and default 3/2 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 189/4 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 8. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +1, maximum 1 charge(s), and necrotic as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 necrotic to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved necrotic damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 necrotic, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 4; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 8. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 8 hours; operational 480 active minutes, or 360 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 465/345 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 9, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Binding Glyphs (ing-133-0-binding_glyphs): quantity 1; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.
- [1] Focusing lens (ing-133-1-focusing_lens): quantity 1; tier 2; source unit 24 gp / 1.0 lb; role tool; consumed=false.
- [2] Luminous Crystal (ing-133-2-luminous_crystal): quantity 3; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [3] infernal marrow (drop-infernal_marrow): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 109 gp / 0.18 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-weapon: finished unit 221 gp, functional repair material 15/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 221/4 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 8. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Imbues one compatible item with a +1 enhancement and 1 charge. Spend a charge to produce a unholy effect appropriate to the item; charges return at dawn.

## Exalted Ember Enchanted Weapon of Hellfire Essence — recipe-136

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +1, maximum 2 charge(s), and fire as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 fire to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved fire damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 fire, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 10; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 17. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 3 days; operational 1440 active minutes, or 1080 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 1425/1065 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=workshop. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["rare recipe drop", "specialist commission", "dungeon library", "successful experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 8, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 15, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Luminous Crystal (ing-136-0-luminous_crystal): quantity 1; tier 5; source unit 200 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [1] Resonance Charge (ing-136-1-resonance_charge): quantity 1; tier 5; source unit 200 gp / 0.1 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.
- [2] Focusing lens (ing-136-2-focusing_lens): quantity 1; tier 5; source unit 600 gp / 1.0 lb; role tool; consumed=false.
- [3] hellfire essence (drop-hellfire_essence): quantity 1; tier 4; source unit 282 gp / 0.33 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 1, 3. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-weapon: finished unit 1056 gp, functional repair material 15/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 264 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 17. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

Imbues one compatible item with a +1 enhancement and 2 charges. Spend a charge to produce a fire effect appropriate to the item; charges return at dawn.

## Superior Ember Staff of Hellfire Essence — recipe-139

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +1, maximum 2 charge(s), and fire as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 fire to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved fire damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 fire, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-staff: Handheld inscribed hardwood rod; not an automatic quarterstaff or class focus. 2 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 48 × 2 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 5; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 13. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 days; operational 960 active minutes, or 720 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 945/705 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=workshop. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["guild trainer", "faction vendor", "uncommon dungeon book", "reverse engineering"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 10, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Focusing lens (ing-139-0-focusing_lens): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 75 gp / 1.0 lb; role tool; consumed=false.
- [1] Stabilizing matrix (ing-139-1-stabilizing_matrix): quantity 5; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role stabilizer; consumed=true.
- [2] Resonance Dust (ing-139-2-resonance_dust): quantity 3; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.05 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.
- [3] hellfire essence (drop-hellfire_essence): quantity 1; tier 4; source unit 282 gp / 0.33 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 2, 3. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

EN-staff: finished unit 693 gp, functional repair material 1/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 693/4 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 13. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Imbues one compatible item with a +1 enhancement and 2 charges. Spend a charge to produce a fire effect appropriate to the item; charges return at dawn.

## Superior Caustic Staff of Acidic Gland — recipe-142

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +1, maximum 2 charge(s), and acid as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +1 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +1 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 1d6 acid to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +1 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved acid damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 1d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +5 nonspell attack, hit 2d6 acid, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-staff: Handheld inscribed hardwood rod; not an automatic quarterstaff or class focus. 2 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 48 × 2 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 6; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 15. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 days; operational 960 active minutes, or 720 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 945/705 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=workshop. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

## Relic Ember Charm of Hellfire Essence — recipe-145

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +2, maximum 3 charge(s), and fire as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +2 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +2 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 2d6 fire to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +2 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved fire damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 2d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +6 nonspell attack, hit 4d6 fire, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-charm: Small handled steel token, no passive wearing effect. 5 gp, 0.1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 2 × 1 × 0.25 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 1, 2, 3, 4. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. ## Exalted Veiled Enchanted Weapon of Void-Warped Eye — recipe-148

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +2, maximum 3 charge(s), and psychic as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +2 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +2 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 2d6 psychic to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +2 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved psychic damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 2d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +6 nonspell attack, hit 4d6 psychic, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

## Mythic Frost Wand of Silver Dragonheart — recipe-151

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +2, maximum 4 charge(s), and cold as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +2 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +2 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 2d6 cold to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +2 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved cold damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 2d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +6 nonspell attack, hit 4d6 cold, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-wand: Handheld hardwood conductor, no ordinary weapon or spell-focus grant. 1 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 12 × 1 × 1 inches. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.
