## Mythic Gale Wand of Refined Air Core — recipe-154

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +2, maximum 4 charge(s), and thunder as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +2 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +2 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 2d6 thunder to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +2 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved thunder damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 2d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +6 nonspell attack, hit 4d6 thunder, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

## Transcendent Stone Enchantment of Cursed Soul Core — recipe-159

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +3, maximum 6 charge(s), and bludgeoning as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +3 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +3 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 3d6 bludgeoning to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +3 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved bludgeoning damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 3d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +7 nonspell attack, hit 6d6 bludgeoning, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-charm: Small handled steel token, no passive wearing effect. 5 gp, 0.1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 2 × 1 × 0.25 inches; EN-weapon: Existing Standard Medium longsword default; other specifically registered eligible ordinary weapon models use their actual source body. 15 gp, 3 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 40 × 8 × 2 inches; EN-armor: Existing Standard Medium chain shirt default; other registered ordinary armor suits retain their exact source body. 50 gp, 20 lb, ordinary B15, structural AC 19, dimensions 30 × 22 × 6 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 20; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 30. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 6 months; operational 57600 active minutes, or 43200 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 57585/43185 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Resonance Circle, with Enchanter's tools, Focusing lens, Inscription stylus; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=legacy. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 3 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["sealed library", "world event", "Gate Guardian cache", "multiseason research"], "minimumResearchHours": 48, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 20, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. **Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

## Sentient Memory Seal — recipe-638

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Each finished body has enhancement +3, maximum 6 charge(s), and psychic as its sole charge damage type. An admitted actual weapon adds +3 to its eligible item accuracy comparison and +3 ordinary flat weapon damage once per hit; its unsuppressed ordinary B/P/S damage is magical. Charged Strike costs one Action and one charge before its single actual weapon attack; a hit adds 3d6 psychic to that attack's damage event. It is not the Attack Action and grants no Extra Attack; an ordinary critical doubles dice, never flat bonuses. An admitted armor suit supplies +3 in equipment.defense-enhancement only while its worn armor formula is selected. Typed Guard spends a Reaction and charge on a positive resolved psychic damage component, after defenses but before HP/positive-damage triggers, reducing it by 3d6 to a minimum of zero. Every other admitted carrier uses Typed Pulse: Action, one charge, one manipulating hand, one perceived creature or physically compatible object within 30 feet and line of effect; fixed +7 nonspell attack, hit 6d6 psychic, no ability/PB/flat enhancement added again. Only the profile belonging to the registered body is available. These activations spend no RP, do not enter spell cadence and cannot be Counterspelled. Charges refill to maximum only at an eligible actual campaign 06:00 dawn under EN-06; suppressed/missing/zero-HP items miss that event, with no catch-up or local-sunrise/transfer reset. Titles supply no unprinted flight, command, healing or class focus. EN-01–10 and AC-07 supply the full use and comparison procedures.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: EN-seal: Handheld steel seal plate, not a door seal or sentient actor. 5 gp, 0.25 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 3 × 3 × 0.25 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Enchanting level 20; INT with actual Enchanter's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 28. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 3 months; operational 28800 active minutes, or 21600 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 28785/21585 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.
